Directory Structure

Collapsible directory tree showing file and symbol counts. Directories with LLM summaries show them inline.

java/5 files · 20 symbols
This directory contains a suite of Java CLI tools for processing BMDExpress 3 project files (.bm2 format), enabling data extraction, format conversion, and statistical analysis in a unified workflow. The tools support exporting genomics and category-level analysis to JSON and TSV, integrating multiple project files, performing prefilter statistical tests (Williams trend test, Dunnett's comparisons), and round-tripping between JSON and native .bm2 formats. Together, they form a data pipeline for dose-response genomics analysis and metadata enrichment.
output/1 files · 1 symbols
This directory contains a utility for generating documentation about the BMDExpress dose-response gene expression analysis pipeline. It uses python-docx to create a formatted Word document (.docx) that explains the interpretation pipeline, including structured headings, paragraphs, tables, and styled text.
pdf_text/3 files · 51 symbols
pdf_text is a from-scratch PDF parser library designed to extract semantic text from PDFs while replacing external PDF dependencies (requiring only zlib). It handles the complete PDF processing pipeline—resolving object graphs, decompressing streams, walking page trees, classifying text by semantic structure, and supporting advanced features like ToUnicode CMaps and character encodings. The package also includes a lossless PDF codec that can decompose PDFs into labeled byte spans for JSON storage and perfectly reconstruct them.
tests/21 files · 272 symbols
The `tests` directory contains the test suite for the project, with pytest configuration and shared fixtures in `conftest.py` that provide test isolation, state management, and mocking for external dependencies such as Java subprocesses, HTTP calls, and LLM APIs. This setup ensures tests run in clean, isolated environments without cross-test contamination.
e2e/4 files · 18 symbols
This directory contains end-to-end tests for a data pipeline system using Pytest and Playwright, with support for visual step-through debugging, screenshots, and structured logging. The tests validate the complete pipeline flow from session loading or data upload through validation, integration, processing, and approval stages using both headless smoke tests and interactive headful browser tests. The test infrastructure provides fixtures for live test server deployment and interactive test inspection capabilities.
fixtures/1 files · 0 symbols
The tests/fixtures directory serves as a Python package for organizing test fixtures and supporting data used across the project's test suite. Currently, it contains only an `__init__.py` file marking it as a package, with fixture modules likely to be added as needed.
integration/6 files · 45 symbols
The `tests/integration` directory contains integration tests for a biomedical data management system, covering file uploads, session lifecycle management (load/approve/unapprove), pool validation and integration endpoints, and complex data processing workflows that generate section cards with tables and summaries across multiple platforms. These tests validate HTTP responses, in-memory caching, domain model correctness (platform names, required JSON fields), and error handling for key API endpoints.
unit/8 files · 198 symbols
The tests/unit directory contains comprehensive unit tests for the project's core modules, covering document tree manipulation, gene expression data filtering and platform-based partitioning, utility functions for dose-response experiments, and session persistence mechanisms. These tests verify the correctness of data structures, transformation logic, and file I/O operations across the codebase.

File Inventory

FileLanguageLinesSymbols
apical_bmds.pypython3294
Implements EPA BMDS continuous model fitting for apical endpoints using pybmds, with parallel processing to produce BMD, BMDL, BMDU estimates and NIEHS classification bins.
background_server.pypython4208
FastAPI application entry point for 5dToxReport that sets up routing, middleware, error handling, and serves a toxicology reporting web UI with chemical resolution and session management endpoints.
background_writer.pypython76610
Generates formal scientific background/introduction sections for toxicology study reports by assembling regulatory and toxicological data into LLM prompts, then generates via Claude API (primary) or local Ollama (fallback).
bmd_project_schema.pypython99319
Defines Pydantic models that validate `integrated.json` at load time, ensuring data conforms to the BMDProject schema before being consumed by downstream code. Handles deduplication of legacy apical experiments and repointing of BMDExpress results to truth siblings.
body_weight_table.pypython6983
Builds NIEHS Table 2 (Body Weights) from sidecar JSON files, computing statistics directly from raw animal-level data and handling animal attrition tracking and footnote generation.
build_db.pypython40810
Builds bmdx.duckdb, a normalized DuckDB analytical database linking papers, genes, GO terms, pathways, and citation networks from multiple JSON and TSV sources. Handles deduplication across multiple citegraph_output variants and normalizes extracted entities (genes, organs).
build_docx.pypython1106
Provides reusable utilities for building formatted DOCX documents with headings, paragraphs, bullet lists, and tables using python-docx.
chem_resolver.pypython49713
Resolves chemical identifiers (names, CASRN, CID, DTXSID) to comprehensive chemical metadata by querying PubChem and EPA CompTox APIs, supporting two-pass resolution with fallback to PubChem-only mode.
citegraph.pypython88328
Citation graph crawler for toxicogenomics research that uses the Semantic Scholar API to expand a network from seed papers, with governor-controlled stopping based on relevance thresholds, saturation detection, and budget limits. Supports general and organ-specific crawls with comprehensive metadata extraction and graph persistence.
clinical_pathology_table.pypython3961
Builds NIEHS clinical pathology tables (Tables 4/5/6) from animal sidecar data and NTP statistical output, combining raw sample counts with mean±SE values, significance markers, and BMD/BMDL estimates for all measured endpoints.
crawl_phase2.pypython4703
Orchestrates a three-tier literature crawl pipeline using targeted searches, gene-aware scoring, and keyword boosting to expand a toxicology knowledge base based on gap analysis from prior interpretation runs.
data_gatherer.pypython111319
Gathers regulatory and toxicological data from multiple public databases (PubChem, EPA CTX/IRIS/SDWA, ATSDR, OSHA/NIOSH, Semantic Scholar, CPDat) for a resolved chemical to populate a BackgroundData object used for writing structured toxicology background sections.
document_tree.pypython57310
Defines the complete structural document tree for an NIEHS 5-day biological potency report, including node type definitions, the full DOCUMENT_TREE hierarchy matching NBK589955's Table of Contents, and utilities for tree traversal, numbering, filtering, and serialization.
enrichr_analysis.pypython2173
CLI tool for running Enrichr enrichment analysis on genes from genomics session files, handling session parsing, API queries across multiple libraries, and JSON output.
enrichr_client.pypython2143
Minimal client for the Enrichr gene set enrichment analysis REST API, providing functions to submit gene lists and fetch enrichment results against various biological databases without external dependencies.
export_routes.pypython3585
FastAPI router providing endpoints for exporting NIEHS Report 10-structured PDFs and managing the global LLM writing style profile.
export_sessions.pypython2366
Utility script that converts Claude Code session JSONL transcripts to readable Markdown format, with special formatting for user messages, assistant responses, and tool usage.
extract.pypython109319
LLM-based extraction of structured information (claims, genes, organs, methods) from scientific papers using parallel Ollama endpoints, with gene normalization and consensus analysis.
extract_phase2_runner.pypython280
Script that runs Phase 2 extraction across 9 crawl directories, processing papers from JSON files and saving extractions.
fulltext.pypython57222
Full-text retrieval system for academic papers from multiple open-access sources (PMC, arXiv, S2, Unpaywall, DOI proxy) with local disk caching and batch processing capabilities.
genefunc_crawl.pypython60414
Implements a second-pass literature crawler for gene biological functions in specific organs using Semantic Scholar. Searches for papers explaining gene-organ relationships (distinct from toxicogenomics vocabulary) and performs citation expansion to build a network of relevant papers.
genomics_narratives.pypython1953
Shared assembler for Gene Set and Gene BMD body narratives that ensures PDF export and HTML rendering display identical per-organ findings paragraphs; provides pure, data-driven construction with no I/O or session dependencies.
genomics_viz.pypython100313
Provides FastAPI endpoints for genomics visualization: hierarchical clustering of GO categories by gene-set overlap and server-side rendering of UMAP/scatter charts as PNG images for report embedding.
go_gene_map.pypython3144
Builds a GO term to gene mapping by merging rat and human gene annotations with UMAP coordinate data, and enriches the output with toxicogenomics evidence and organ metadata.
interpret.pypython238852
Dose-response interpretation engine that analyzes gene-level BMD results from BMDExpress by querying a toxicogenomics knowledge base to produce grounded, literature-backed narratives via LLM synthesis.
java/ExportBm2.javajava1032
Unified .bm2 export utility that deserializes BMDExpress 3 project files and produces both JSON (via Jackson) and category analysis TSV (via BMDExpress services) in a single JVM invocation, replacing the previous 3-JVM approach.
java/ExportCategories.javajava3224
Extracts category analysis BMD statistics from BMDExpress 3 .bm2 files and exports them as JSON, replacing a broken TSV export mechanism. Handles deserialization, statistical aggregation, and direction inference for multiple category types (DEFINED, Gene Ontology, REACTOME, etc.).
java/ExportGenomics.javajava3553
Extracts gene-level BMD and GO Biological Process category results from BMDExpress 3 .bm2 files, parsing per-probe results and category analysis into a structured JSON output.
java/IntegrateProject.javajava2808
IntegrateProject merges multiple BMDExpress data files (.bm2, .txt/.csv) into a single unified BMDProject, optionally enriches experiments with LLM-inferred metadata from a JSON sidecar, and serializes the result to JSON with custom NaN-to-null handling. Leverages BMDExpress's native ProjectUtilities and ExperimentFileUtil for merge and parsing operations.
java/RunPrefilter.javajava3743
Standalone Java CLI tool that performs dose-response statistical analysis (Williams trend test and Dunnett's pairwise comparisons) on JSON input, using the same sciome-commons-math library as BMDExpress, and outputs statistical results (p-values, fold changes, NOEL/LOEL) as JSON.
llm_helpers.pypython1213
Provides shared utilities for calling Claude API and generating JSON responses. Includes a markdown-fence stripper for cleaning LLM output and both synchronous and asynchronous wrappers around AnthropicEndpoint for JSON generation.
llm_routes.pypython118410
FastAPI endpoints for LLM-powered generation of toxicology report sections (background, methods, summary, genomics narratives) with SSE streaming, data extraction, and optional enrichment.
